Let's derive a linear least-squares iterative update more rigorously.

Let x∈R_+^n, normalized S=1. Let C = conv(x,x) length m=2n-1. Define objective P = max C.
We work in log domain y_i = log x_i (with zeros problematic). Update x_new = normalize(exp(y - τ g)), where g length n. Then to first order:
C_new ≈ C - 2τ (x * g)   (full convolution length 2n-1).
Here convolution is same as evaluator. Because x*g coefficient r =∑ x_i g_{r-i}; derivative of C wrt y: dC_r/τ = -2∑ x_i x_{j} (g_i+g_j) with j=r-i? Wait if y_i+δ_i, x_i' = x_i(1+δ_i) where δ_i=-τ g_i. Then C_r' = ∑ x_i x_j (δ_i+δ_j) over i+j=r = ∑ (x_i δ_{r-i} + x_{r-i} δ_i) = 2∑ x_i δ_{r-i} = -2τ (x*g)_r. Yes.

Thus to first order we can choose g to achieve desired decrease D_r = 2τ (x*g)_r. If we want new C = α C + (1-α) T (scale toward target) then choose D = C - [α C +(1-α)T] = (1-α)(C-T). Therefore we need solve low-order linear equation x*g ≈ (1/(2τ))(C-T) (with scale). Since τ can be absorbed, we want g such that x*g approximates R = C - T. We can solve least squares for g.     m=2*n-1

    C = irfft(X*X, N)[:m].real

    # maybe clip C tiny neg

Let's design the iteration function `optimize_sequence(x, n, N, aim_score, start_time, max_time, rng)` returns best_x,best_score, score_history.

Detailed loop:
```
def optimize_sequence(x, aim_score, deadline, floor=1e-10, eps=1e-8, max_log=6.0, base_steps=(0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,0.015625)):
    x = np.asarray(x,float).copy()
    x = np.clip(x, floor, None)
    if x.sum()<=0: x=np.ones(n)*...
    x /= x.sum()
    n = len(x)
    m=2*n-1
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=x.copy(); best_score=inf; current_score=inf; no_improve=0
    step_tune = 0.5
    while time < deadline:
        X = rfft(x,N)
        C = irfft(X*X,N)[:m].real
        C = 0.5*(C + C[::-1])
        # clip small neg:
        C = np.where(C < 0, 0.0, C) maybe loses sum. Better if min > -1e-9 set max(0). Use np.maximum(C,0).
        csum=C.sum()
        if not finite or csum<=0: break
        C = C/csum
        M=C.max(); score=2*n*M
        if score<best_score: update best; no_improve=0 else no_improve++
        if score < aim_score and no_improve? maybe continue with lower aim?
        target_M = max(aim_score/(2*n), 0)
        if M <= target_M * 1.001:
            # polish: T = 0.98*M maybe, but if no improve break
            T = 0.98*M
        else:
            T = target_M
            # maybe if M/target_M > 1.2, use T=0.8/M? But direct target okay.
        R = C - T
        # Could subtract R.mean()? The scale component? For derivative with natural h and normalization, constant part? Since we center h weighted mean, it removes constant log, not necessarily R mean. Use R as is.
        Rf = rfft(R,N)
        Xabs2 = X.real*X.real + X.imag*X.imag
        lam = 1e-8*Xabs2.max()
        D = np.conj(X)*Rf/(Xabs2+lam)
        D[0] /= D? no.
        d = irfft(D,N)[:n].real
        # maybe remove component that is just scaling? We can subtract np.dot(x,d)? Since h=d/x; weighted mean h =∑d. Subtract weighted mean by d -= np.dot(x,d) * x? Wait h_wmean=∑ x_i h_i = ∑ d_i. To make h weighted mean zero, we can d_new = d - (∑d) x? Because h_new=(d - c x)/x = h - c. Weighted mean h_new=∑(h)-c=0. So subtract c x from d. This removes pure scaling direction. Good! c = np.sum(d) (since sum x=1, weighted mean of h = ∑d). So d = d - (np.sum(d))*x. This makes h weighted mean zero after division. It removes additive component proportional to x, i.e., scale direction. Great.
        # Also maybe clip d? no.
        h = d / (x + eps)
        h = np.clip(h, -hcap, hcap) where hcap = max_log/step_min? If we later normalize h by sd, we can cap before.
        h -= np.dot(x,h) (should ~0 but due clip)
        sd = sqrt(np.dot(x,h*h))
        if sd<1e-12: break
        g = h/sd
        # Try alphas. Need max_log per step. We can use max_log=4.0. yclip.

           x_new = np.clip(x_new, floor, 10.0)
           x_new = x_new/x_new.sum()
           # compute score fast

           if finite and score_new < best_c_score: update best_cand
           if score_new < score: break? maybe enough; but larger alpha might also better? Usually smaller safer. We can choose best among tried; start from moderate? Now, score_from_x: Use FFT. We can inline to avoid function overhead? Helper top-level.
```
def fast_score(x, N, n):
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(X*X, N)[:2*n-1].real
    # sym
    C = 0.5*(C + C[::-1])
    C = np.maximum(C,0.0)
    s=C.sum()
    if s>0: C=C/s
    return 2*n*float(np.max(C))
```

def random_perturb(x, rng):
   n=x.size
   scale=0.1
   # global multiplicative noise
   x = x * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))
   # maybe symmetrize with prob?

   x=np.clip(x, 1e-10, None); x/=x.sum()
   return x
```

`normalize_sequence`: If v size 0, return np.ones(16). Ensure floor but if all clipped zero, use uniform. Use `np.nan_to_num`.
```
def normalize_sequence(v, floor=1e-10, max_entry=100.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        v = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=max_entry, neginf=0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, max_entry)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        v = np.ones_like(v)
    else:
        v = v / s
    v = np.maximum(v, floor)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if s > 0: v = v / s
    return v
```
Floor 1e-10, max_entry 100. If v size large, adding floor to all changes sum by n*1e-10, negligible. If n=10000, 1e-6.

`downsample_to_length(v,L)` top-level:
```
def downsample_to_length(v,L):
  v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float).ravel()
  n=v.size
  if n<=0: return None
  if n==L: return v
  if n<L:
    out=np.zeros(L); out[:n]=v; return out
  blocks=(n+L-1)//L
  out=np.zeros(L)
  start=0
  for i in range(L):
     end=min(n,start+blocks)
     if end>start: out[i]=float(v[start:end].mean())
     start=end
  return out
```
Loop 8192 okay. Could vectorize but top-level no lambda. Use loop.

`random_start` maybe using scipy? no. Use rng.

```
def make_random_start(n, rng, sym=True):
   base = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, n))
   # smooth with simple average over random window
   w = int(max(2, n // 200))
   if w < n:
      kernel = np.ones(w, dtype=float); kernel /= w
      # direct convolve length small kernel and n large: O(nw) maybe 5582*27=150k okay
      base = np.convolve(base, kernel, mode='same')
   base = np.maximum(base, 1e-6)
   if sym: base = 0.5*(base + base[::-1])
   return normalize_sequence(base)
```
`np.convolve` with kernel 27 direct O150k, okay. For n=8192 w=41, okay.

def random_perturb(x, rng, strength=0.15):
   n=x.size
   cand=x.copy()
   # maybe two blocks
   for _ in range(rng.integers(1,4)):
      w=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//1000), max(2,n//20)))
      s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
      cand[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, w))
   if rng.random() < 0.4:
      cand = 0.5*(cand + cand[::-1])
   return normalize_sequence(cand)
```

```
def fft_conv_data(x,n,N):
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    Cf = X * X
    C = np.fft.irfft(Cf, N)[:2*n-1].real
    C = 0.5*(C + C[::-1])
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
    s = float(C.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
       C = C / s
    return X, Cf, C
```
Then in optimize call. But small overhead. Top-level. It modifies C in place. Good.

Need `fast_score` use `fft_conv_data`:
```
def fast_score(x,n,N):
    _,_,C = fft_conv_data(x,n,N)
    if C.size==0: return inf
    return 2.0*n*float(np.max(C))
```

In optimize loop:
```
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(x,n,N)
    cur = best_score
    alpha = 0.5
    no_improve=0; iters=0; last_print=0
    maxlog=4.0; max_entry=10.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       X, Cf, C = fft_conv_data(x,n,N)
       M = float(np.max(C))
       score = 2*n*M
       if not finite: break
       if score < best_score: best score update
       if score > cur +? cur=score (current x)
       # choose T
       meanC = 1.0/m
       if M <= meanC * 1.001: break
       aim_M = aim_score/(2.0*n)
       if M > aim_M:
          T = aim_M
       else:
          T = max(meanC, 0.97*M)
       Rf = Cf - T*ones_fft
       Xabs2 = X.real*X.real + X.imag*X.imag
       maxX2 = float(np.max(Xabs2))
       lam = 1e-8*maxX2 if maxX2>0 else 0.0
       D = np.conj(X) * Rf
       if lam >0: D = D/(Xabs2+lam)
       else: D = D/(Xabs2 + 1e-300)
       # remove mean scaling? We'll compute d then c.
       d = np.fft.irfft(D, N)[:n].real
       if not finite all: break
       c = float(np.sum(d))
       d = d - c*x
       h = d / np.maximum(x, 1e-8)
       h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
       h = np.clip(h, -100.0, 100.0)
       h -= float(np.dot(x,h))
       sd2 = float(np.dot(x, h*h))
       if not finite or sd2 < 1e-14:
          no_improve += 1
          ... restart if
          continue
       g = h / math.sqrt(sd2)
       # candidate list
       cands = []
       for a in (min(2.5, alpha*1.6), alpha, 0.6*alpha, 0.3*alpha, 0.15*alpha, 0.05, 0.2):
          if a > 1e-4 and a < 3.0:
             # unique
             if not any(abs(a-b) <= 1e-8 for b in cands): cands.append(a)
       cands.sort(reverse=True)
       if len(cands) > 5: cands = cands[:5]
       best_cand=None; best_c_score=score; best_a=alpha
       for a in cands:
          y = -a*g
          y = np.clip(y, -maxlog, maxlog)
          xn = x * np.exp(y)
          xn = np.clip(xn, floor, max_entry)
          ssum = float(np.sum(xn))
          if not finite or ssum<=0: continue
          xn = xn / ssum
          sc = fast_score(xn,n,N)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_c_score:
             best_cand=xn; best_c_score=sc; best_a=a
       if best_cand is not None and best_c_score < score - 1e-12:
           x=best_cand; alpha=best_a
           no_improve=0
           if x is best? update best...
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           alpha *= 0.7
           if alpha < 1e-4: alpha=1e-4
           if no_improve > 25:
              x = random_perturb(best_x, rng)
              alpha = 0.3
              no_improve=0
       iters +=1
       if iters % 100 ==0 or (best_score-printed >...): print
       if best_score <= aim_score * 0.999 and no_improve>50: break
    return best_x, best_score
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except: budget=2400.0
    budget=min(budget, 2350.0)
    deadline=t0+budget
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed if seed is not None else 2024)
    # get base
    base = None
    try:
       raw = height_sequence_1
       v = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if v.size>0:
          base = v
    except Exception: pass
    MAX_N=8192
    if base is not None:
       n = base.size
       if n > MAX_N:
          base = downsample_to_length(base, MAX_N); n=MAX_N
       elif n < 16:
          base = None
    if base is None:
       n = 5582
       base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # ensure length max? if n > MAX_N maybe downsample; if n small set to 64/5582
    n = int(round(n))
    if n < 16: n=5582; base=np.ones(n)
    if n > MAX_N: base=downsample_to_length(base,MAX_N); n=MAX_N
    # starts
    starts=[]
    bw = np.clip(base,0,None)
    b_sum = bw.sum(); if b_sum>0: bw/=b_sum
    starts.append(('base_sym', normalize_sequence(0.5*(bw+bw[::-1]))))
    starts.append(('uniform', normalize_sequence(np.ones(n))))
    starts.append(('rand1', make_random_start(n, rng, sym=True)))
    # endpoint shape if time
    t = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    endpoint = (t+1e-4)**(-0.9) + (1.0-t+1e-4)**(-0.9)
    starts.append(('endpoint', normalize_sequence(0.5*(endpoint+endpoint[::-1])))) maybe endpoint already sym.
    # evaluate fast to choose initial best
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best_seq=None; best_score=inf
    for name, seq in starts:
       sc=fast_score(seq,n,N)
       print("start",name,"score",sc)
       if sc<best_score: best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=sc
    # Stage A
    stage_deadline = t0 + 0.40*budget
    if time.monotonic() < stage_deadline:
       x,s = optimize_sequence(best_seq, 1.65, stage_deadline, seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1)), tag="A")
       if s<best_score: best_score=s; best_seq=x
       print stage end best
    # exploration random
    stage_deadline2 = t0 + min(budget*0.58, ...)
    if time < deadline and best_score > 1.5? maybe
       alt = make_random_start(n, rng, sym=False)
       x,s = optimize_sequence(alt, 1.55, deadline2, tag="B")
       update
    # Polishing
    stage_deadline3 = t0 + budget*0.92
    if time<deadline3:
       x,s=optimize_sequence(best_seq, 1.495, deadline3, tag="C")
       update
    # local search remaining 8% if >10s
    if best_seq is not None and time < deadline-5:
       local_deadline = max(time.monotonic()+5, deadline-3)
       best_seq = local_block_search(best_seq, local_deadline, int(rng.integers(...)))
       # recompute best_score? local returns score
    # finalize
    return final_sequence(best_seq, deadline)
```

def finalize_sequence(x, deadline):

   n=x.size; N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   variants=[]
   variants.append((1.0, x))
   sym=0.5*(x+x[::-1]); variants.append((2.0, normalize_sequence(sym)))
   z=x.copy(); z[z<1e-8]=0; variants.append((3.0, normalize_sequence(z)))
   zs=0.5*(z+z[::-1]); variants.append((4.0, normalize_sequence(zs)))
   best_internal=... fast_score
   candidates=[best_internal first]
   # remove duplicates maybe
   # time for direct: if budget left > 3s
   top = candidates sorted by internal
   if len(top)>3: top=top[:3]
   best_actual=None; best_actual_score=np.inf
   for seq in top:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0: break
      try:
         sc=float(evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist()))
      except Exception:
         sc=np.inf
      if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_actual_score:
         best_actual=seq; best_actual_score=sc
      # maybe can break if score very low
   if best_actual is not None:
      return [float(val) for val in best_actual]
   else:
      return [float(val) for val in candidates[0][1]]
```

Detailed function `fft_conv_data`:
```
def fft_conv_data(x, n, N):
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    Cf = X * X
    C = np.fft.irfft(Cf, N)[:2*n-1].real
    C = 0.5 * (C + C[::-1])
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
    s = float(np.sum(C))
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        C = C / s
        Cf = Cf / s
    return X, Cf, C
```
If C sum zero, Cf not normalized; but fail.

`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(x,n,N):
    if x is None: return inf
    _,_,C=fft_conv_data(x,n,N)
    if C.size==0: return inf
    maxc=float(np.max(C))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <=0: return inf
    return float(2.0*n*maxc)
```
`fft_conv_data` normalizes C sum; if x sum not 1? The score formula would be off if x not normalized. But before fast_score, sequences normalized. In local variants, normalize. Good. Could `fft_conv_data` not scale by x sum? Since x might sum not1 if Normalization failed; but we ensure. In final internal variants, normalize. In candidate exact, normalize. In fast_score for starts, normalize. Good.

If someone calls fast_score with unnormalized, Csum=(sum x)^2 not1; our normalization makes score independent of scale, equivalent to objective. So fine! Normalizing C to sum1 effectively normalizes by S^2 automatically, even if x sum not1. Great. Thus fast_score robust. Then we don't strictly need x sum1 for score, but updates rely. Normalize x still.

In `fft_conv_data`, C normalized by its sum. If x has zeros and Csum=0, invalid. Good.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try: budget = float(budget_s)
    except: budget = 2400.0
    if budget < 60: budget = 60.0
    budget = min(budget, 2350.0)
    hard_deadline = t0 + budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed if seed is not None else 2023)
    # load base ...
    starts...
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    for name, sq in starts:
       sc=fast_score(sq,n,N)
       print(f"start {name} score {sc}")
       if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_seq=sq.copy()
    # Stage A
    stage_end = t0 + 0.40*budget
    if best_score <= 1.65:
       stage_end = min(stage_end, t0 + 0.10*budget)
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 10:
       x,s = optimize_sequence(best_seq, 1.65, stage_end, seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1)), tag="A")
       if s<best_score: best_score=s; best_seq=x
       print("A done", best_score)
    # Stage B exploration if time and not already low
    rem_end = t0 + budget
    if best_score > 1.50:
       b_end = t0 + 0.55*budget
    else:
       b_end = min(t0 + 0.50*budget, t0 + 0.08*budget)
    if time.monotonic() < min(b_end, hard_deadline-10):
       alt = make_random_start(n, rng, sym=False)
       x,s = optimize_sequence(alt, 1.55, b_end, seed=..., tag="B")
       update
    # Stage C polish
    # use aim 1.495
    if best_score <= 1.495:
       c_end = min(t0 + 0.93*budget, t0 + 0.10*budget)
    else:
       c_end = t0 + 0.93*budget
    if time.monotonic() < min(c_end, hard_deadline-10):
       x,s = optimize_sequence(best_seq, 1.495, c_end, seed=..., tag="C")
       update
    # local search if remaining > 20s
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() + 20 < hard_deadline:
       local_end = max(time.monotonic()+5, min(hard_deadline-5, t0 + budget))
       best_seq, best_score = local_block_search(best_seq, local_end, int(rng.integers(...)))
       print("local best", best_score)
    # finalize
    seq = finalize_sequence(best_seq, hard_deadline - 2)
    return seq
```

Alternative algorithm: active set leveling using linear programming? The prior SOTA described solving LP maximizing sum b subject to ||f*g||∞≤M. Could implement? Maybe more powerful? Let's revisit.

Given f (current), they compute g0 by LP maximizing ∑ b_j subject b_j≥0 and (f*g0)_k ≤ M (where M=||f*f||∞). Since f fixed, constraints are linear in b_j: each (f*g0)_k = ∑ f_i g_{k-i} ≤ M. This LP finds a nonnegative b with high sum under convolution with f bounded by M. Then rescale to sum √(2n) and update f convex. This is an extreme point search, maybe more global. Could be implemented with scipy.optimize.linprog? n=5582 constraints 2n-1, variables n. LP dense? Each constraint banded with f_i. Could use scipy? Too large maybe. But could be inspired: We can solve via projected subgradient for b: maximize sum b subject to f*b≤M and b≥0. This is a linear program with interval matrix (Toeplitz/f). The optimal b likely puts mass where constraints not active. We can update f towards b. This is like classic "shift toward LP solution". Could improve beyond our gradient.

Can we approximate LP with iterative scaling (Picard) for fixed f? Variables b_j≥0. Constraints f*b_k ≤ M. We want max 1^T b. This is a fractional packing LP. A fast method: initialize b uniform; iterate b_j ← b_j * exp(-ν * active_violation?)? Constraints are convolution of f and b. The Lagrangian max ∑b_j - ∑_k λ_k (f*b)_k, λ≥0. Optimal b nonzero for coordinates where 1 - ∑_k λ_k f_{?} ≤? The subgradient for b_j is 1 - (f^T λ)_j, where (f^T λ)_j =∑_k λ_k f_{k-j}. So use multiplicative update b_j ∝ exp(η(1 - ∑ λ_k f_{k-j})) with λ updated to reduce violations f*b - M. This is dual ascent. Could solve LP approximately in O(n log n) per iteration using FFT. This is interesting.

Double-check functions:

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    if x <= 1: return 1
    n = 1
    while n < x:
        n <<= 1
    return n
```

`make_random_start`: uses `rng.normal`. If `rng` not passed? We'll pass.

`random_perturb`: uses `rng.integers`. Ensure width at least 1 and stop. If n small.
```
def random_perturb(x, rng, strength=0.15):
   n=x.size
   cand=x.copy()
   nblocks=int(rng.integers(1,4))
   for _ in range(nblocks):
      minw=1; maxw=max(2,n//20)
      if maxw<minw: maxw=minw
      w=int(rng.integers(minw, maxw+1))
      s=int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1))
      cand[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, w))
   if rng.random()<0.4:
      cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
   return normalize_sequence(cand)
```

`local_block_search`: similar but weaker strength and sym.
```
def local_block_search(x, deadline, seed=0):
   x=normalize_sequence(x)
   n=x.size; N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   best=x.copy(); best_sc=fast_score(...)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   strength=0.08; no=0; it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      cand=random_perturb(best,rng,strength)
      sc=fast_score(cand,n,N)
      if sc < best_sc -1e-12:
         best=cand; best_sc=sc; no=0
         if it%1000==0: print
      else:
         no+=1
         if no>200:
            strength=min(0.3, strength*1.2)
            no=0
      it+=1
   return best,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Search for a low-peak nonnegative convolution square.

The objective is
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a)**2)
for a nonnegative sequence a.  This module uses a sequence of staged
natural-gradient / least-squares deconvolution steps in the log domain,
with FFT-based internal scoring and a small local random polish at the
end.  The update solves, for a target coefficient T, a least-squares
problem x*d ~= (conv(x,x)-T), then converts d to a multiplicative
log-direction d/x and performs a line search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def next_pow2(x):
    """Smallest power of two >= x (at least 1)."""
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    n = 1
    while n < x:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def normalize_sequence(v, floor=1e-10, max_entry=100.0):
    """Clean and normalize a vector to a valid nonnegative probability-like vector."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        v = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, max_entry)

    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        v = np.ones_like(v, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        v = v / s

    v = np.maximum(v, floor)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        v = v / s
    return v


def downsample_to_length(v, L):
    """Downsample a sequence to length L by block averaging."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == L:
        return v.copy()
    if n < L:
        out = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        out[:n] = v
        return out

    blocks = (n + L - 1) // L
    out = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    start = 0
    for i in range(L):
        end = min(n, start + blocks)
        if end > start:
            out[i] = float(np.mean(v[start:end]))
        start = end
    return out


def make_random_start(n, rng, sym=True):
    """Random smoothed positive starting point."""
    base = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, n))

    w = int(max(2, n // 200))
    if w < n:
        kernel = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64)
        kernel /= float(w)
        base = np.convolve(base, kernel, mode="same")

    base = np.maximum(base, 1e-6)
    if sym:
        base = 0.5 * (base + base[::-1])
    return normalize_sequence(base)


def fft_conv_data(x, n, N):
    """Zero-padded FFT convolution data for x*x, normalized to sum 1."""
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    Cf = X * X
    C = np.fft.irfft(Cf, N)[:2 * n - 1].real

    C = 0.5 * (C + C[::-1])
    np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)

    s = float(np.sum(C))
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        C = C / s
        Cf = Cf / s

    return X, Cf, C


def fast_score(x, n, N):
    """FFT-based version of the objective, scale-invariant."""
    if x is None or n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    _, _, C = fft_conv_data(x, n, N)
    if C.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    maxc = float(np.max(C))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc)


def optimize_sequence(x0, aim_score, deadline, seed=0, floor=1e-10,
                      max_random_restarts=35, tag="opt"):
    """
    Staged least-squares natural gradient optimizer.

    Returns (best_sequence, best_internal_score).
    """
    x = normalize_sequence(x0, floor=floor)
    n = int(x.size)
    if n < 4:
        x = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        n = 64

    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(m)

    ones_fft = np.zeros(N // 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    ones_fft[0] = float(N)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))

    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(x, n, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        x = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        best_x = x.copy()
        best_score = fast_score(x, n, N)

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best_x, best_score

    alpha = 0.5
    no_improve = 0
    iteration = 0
    restarts = 0
    exploration_left = 0
    maxlog = 4.0
    max_entry = 10.0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        X, Cf, C = fft_conv_data(x, n, N)
        if C.size == 0:
            break

        M = float(np.max(C))
        score = float(2 * n * M)
        if not np.isfinite(score) or score <= 0.0:
            break

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_x = x.copy()

        meanC = 1.0 / float(m)
        if M <= meanC * 1.001:
            break

        aim_M = float(aim_score) / (2.0 * float(n))
        if M > aim_M:
            T = aim_M
        else:
            T = max(meanC, 0.97 * M)

        if not np.isfinite(T) or T <= 0.0:
            break

        Rf = Cf - T * ones_fft
        Xabs2 = X.real * X.real + X.imag * X.imag
        maxX2 = float(np.max(Xabs2))
        if not np.isfinite(maxX2) or maxX2 <= 0.0:
            break

        lam = 1e-8 * maxX2
        D = np.conj(X) * Rf / (Xabs2 + lam)
        d = np.fft.irfft(D, N)[:n].real
        d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)

        # Remove the pure re-scaling component: h = d/x, so constant in h
        # corresponds to d = constant * x.
        c = float(np.sum(d))
        if np.isfinite(c):
            d = d - c * x

        h = d / np.maximum(x, 1e-8)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.clip(h, -100.0, 100.0)
        h -= float(np.dot(x, h))

        sd2 = float(np.dot(x, h * h))
        if not np.isfinite(sd2) or sd2 < 1e-14:
            no_improve += 1
            alpha *= 0.75
            if alpha < 1e-4:
                alpha = 1e-3
            if no_improve > 100:
                break
            continue

        g = h / math.sqrt(sd2)

        raw_alphas = [
            min(2.5, alpha * 1.6),
            alpha,
            0.6 * alpha,
            0.3 * alpha,
            0.15 * alpha,
            0.05,
            0.2
        ]
        alphas = []
        for a in raw_alphas:
            if not np.isfinite(a):
                continue
            if a <= 1e-4 or a >= 3.0:
                continue
            dup = False
            for b in alphas:
                if abs(a - b) <= 1e-8:
                    dup = True
                    break
            if not dup:
                alphas.append(a)
        alphas.sort(reverse=True)
        if len(alphas) > 5:
            alphas = alphas[:5]

        current_score = score
        best_cand = None
        best_cand_score = current_score
        best_alpha = alpha

        for a in alphas:
            y = -a * g
            y = np.clip(y, -maxlog, maxlog)
            xn = x * np.exp(y)
            xn = np.clip(xn, floor, max_entry)
            ssum = float(np.sum(xn))
            if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
                continue
            xn = xn / ssum
            sc = fast_score(xn, n, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score:
                best_cand = xn
                best_cand_score = sc
                best_alpha = a

        iteration += 1

        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_score < current_score - 1e-12:
            x = best_cand
            if best_cand_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = best_cand_score
                best_x = x.copy()
            alpha = max(1e-4, best_alpha)
            no_improve = 0
            improved = True
        else:
            improved = False
            no_improve += 1

            if exploration_left > 0:
                exploration_left -= 1
            else:
                if best_score < current_score - 1e-8 and no_improve > 30:
                    x = best_x.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    alpha = 0.5
                elif no_improve > 50 and restarts < max_random_restarts:
                    x = random_perturb(best_x, rng, strength=0.20)
                    exploration_left = 40
                    no_improve = 0
                    restarts += 1
                    alpha = 0.30
                else:
                    alpha *= 0.75
                    if alpha < 1e-4:
                        alpha = 1e-3

            if no_improve > 250 and restarts >= max_random_restarts:
                break

        if iteration % 200 == 0:
            print(f"{tag}: iter={iteration} current={score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")

    if best_score > fast_score(x, n, N):
        best_x = x.copy()
        best_score = fast_score(x, n, N)

    return best_x, best_score


def random_perturb(x, rng, strength=0.15):
    """Small nonnegative multiplicative block perturbation."""
    n = int(x.size)
    cand = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
    for _ in range(nblocks):
        minw = 1
        maxw = max(2, n // 20)
        if maxw < minw:
            maxw = minw
        w = int(rng.integers(minw, maxw + 1))
        s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
        stop = min(n, s + w)
        if stop > s:
            cand[s:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, stop - s))

    if rng.random() < 0.4:
        cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])

    return normalize_sequence(cand)


def local_block_search(x, deadline, seed=0):
    """Modest random local search used as final polish."""
    x = normalize_sequence(x)
    n = int(x.size)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best = x.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(best, n, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))
    strength = 0.08
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = random_perturb(best, rng, strength=strength)
        sc = fast_score(cand, n, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 250:
                strength = min(0.30, strength * 1.15)
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 500 == 0:
            print(f"local: iter={it} best={best_score:.6f}")

    return best, best_score


def finalize_sequence(x, deadline):
    """Produce the final list, trying a few harmless variants."""
    if x is None:
        x = np.ones(5582, dtype=np.float64)

    x = normalize_sequence(x)
    n = int(x.size)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    variants = []

    v0 = x.copy()
    variants.append(v0)

    v1 = normalize_sequence(0.5 * (x + x[::-1]))
    variants.append(v1)

    z = x.copy()
    z[z < 1e-8] = 0.0
    v2 = normalize_sequence(z)
    variants.append(v2)

    v3 = normalize_sequence(0.5 * (z + z[::-1]))
    variants.append(v3)

    scored = []
    for v in variants:
        sc = fast_score(v, n, N)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            sc = float("inf")
        scored.append((sc, v))

    # Manual insertion sort to avoid lambdas.
    order = list(range(len(scored)))
    for i in range(1, len(order)):
        ai = order[i]
        val_i = scored[ai][0]
        j = i - 1
        while j >= 0 and scored[order[j]][0] > val_i:
            order[j + 1] = order[j]
            j -= 1
        order[j + 1] = ai

    internal_best = scored[order[0]][1]

    # Use the official evaluator only near the end, and sparingly.
    direct_best = internal_best
    direct_best_score = float("inf")

    max_direct = 1 if n > 6000 else 2
    for idx in order[:max_direct]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
            break
        seq = scored[idx][1]
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < direct_best_score:
            direct_best_score = sc
            direct_best = seq

    out = np.asarray(direct_best, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(v) for v in out]


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Uses staged optimization and a final local polish.
    Returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0

    budget = min(max(budget, 60.0), 2350.0)
    hard_deadline = t0 + budget

    if seed is None:
        seed = 2024
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))

    # Base sequence from the supplied global if available.
    base = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        v = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if v.size > 0:
            base = v
    except Exception:
        base = None

    MAX_N = 8192
    if base is not None:
        if base.size > MAX_N:
            base = downsample_to_length(base, MAX_N)
        if base.size < 16:
            base = None

    if base is None:
        base = np.ones(5582, dtype=np.float64)

    n = int(base.size)
    if n < 16:
        n = 5582
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > MAX_N:
        base = downsample_to_length(base, MAX_N)
        n = MAX_N

    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    # Starting candidates.
    b = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0)
    sb = float(np.sum(b))
    if sb > 0.0 and np.isfinite(sb):
        b = b / sb
    else:
        b = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    starts = []
    starts.append(("base_sym", normalize_sequence(0.5 * (b + b[::-1]))))
    starts.append(("uniform", normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))))
    starts.append(("rand1", make_random_start(n, rng, sym=True)))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    endpoint = (t + 1e-4) ** (-0.9) + (1.0 - t + 1e-4) ** (-0.9)
    endpoint = 0.5 * (endpoint + endpoint[::-1])
    starts.append(("endpoint", normalize_sequence(endpoint)))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    for name, sq in starts:
        sc = fast_score(sq, n, N)
        print(f"start {name}: score={sc:.6f}")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = sq.copy()

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        best_score = fast_score(best_seq, n, N)

    # Stage A: coarse leveling.
    stage_end = t0 + 0.40 * budget
    if best_score <= 1.65:
        stage_end = min(stage_end, t0 + 0.10 * budget)

    if time.monotonic() < min(stage_end, hard_deadline - 10.0):
        x, s = optimize_sequence(best_seq, 1.65, stage_end,
                                 seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31)), tag="A")
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best_score = s
            best_seq = x
        print(f"stage A done: best={best_score:.6f}")

    # Stage B: alternate basin exploration if still needed.
    if best_score > 1.50:
        b_end = t0 + 0.55 * budget
    else:
        b_end = min(t0 + 0.50 * budget, t0 + 0.08 * budget)

    if time.monotonic() < min(b_end, hard_deadline - 10.0):
        alt = make_random_start(n, rng, sym=False)
        x, s = optimize_sequence(alt, 1.55, b_end,
                                 seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31)), tag="B")
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best_score = s
            best_seq = x
        print(f"stage B done: best={best_score:.6f}")

    # Stage C: strict polishing.
    if best_score <= 1.495:
        c_end = min(t0 + 0.93 * budget, t0 + 0.10 * budget)
    else:
        c_end = t0 + 0.93 * budget

    if time.monotonic() < min(c_end, hard_deadline - 10.0):
        x, s = optimize_sequence(best_seq, 1.495, c_end,
                                 seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31)), tag="C")
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best_score = s
            best_seq = x
        print(f"stage C done: best={best_score:.6f}")

    # Final local random polish, if time remains.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() + 20.0 < hard_deadline:
        local_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, hard_deadline - 5.0)
        best_seq, s2 = local_block_search(best_seq, local_deadline,
                                          seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31)))
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = s2
        print(f"local done: best={best_score:.6f}")

    return finalize_sequence(best_seq, hard_deadline)
```
